# Chronological kernel: an explicit local square decomposition

Status: **unverified proof draft**, fifth launch, first continuation round.
There is a complete algebraic factorization below, including the constant
gradient column and the original initial-position variable. Its coefficient
signs have passed an outward-interval evaluation at the actual depth128.
This is not a uniform proof for all m>=128, a verifier-accepted fact, or a
solution of the original minimax problem. No fact_submit was allocated or used.

The new theoretical question is the sign of explicitly displayed scalar
coefficients in the birth coordinates. This is a sufficient condition for
the old covariance inequality; equivalence of their positivity is NOT claimed.

## 1. Original word, shared data, and exact certificate identity

For an integer m>=128 set epsilon=1/m, lambda=1+sqrt(2),
c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=(1-epsilon)/c, d_j=1+a*c^j, and
H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,d_j,H_j). Let q=2^m-2 and let h_0,...,h_(q-1)
be H_m with its final d_0 removed. Define s_i=sum_(r<i)h_r and S=s_q.
For real vectors v,w_0,...,w_(m-1), all using one arbitrary common Gram,
put p_ij=product_(r<i)(1-h_r/d_j), g_i=v+sum_j p_ij*w_j, and
x_i=x_0-sum_(r<i)h_r*g_r. The first d_j kills its column at query2^j.
Thus g_q=v and x_q=x_0-S*v-sum_j d_j*w_j.

For one common array of sampled values and the zero optimum jet, use
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2. Necessity and sufficiency
of the entire interpolation array are in accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290.
Define, purely algebraically,

 J=sum_(i<q) [2s_(i+1)h_i<g_(i+1),g_i>
             +s_(i+1)||g_(i+1)-g_i||^2+h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2].

The GD norm identity and summation by parts give

 ||x_0||^2-2S*f_q+2sum_i h_i Q_(i,star)
                  +2sum_i s_(i+1)Q_(i+1,i)=||x_q||^2+J.       (1)

Indeed the optimum terms equal
2sum h_i f_i-||x_0||^2+||x_q||^2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2;
the chain terms equal 2S f_q-2sum h_i f_i plus the first two terms of J.
All selected multipliers are nonnegative. No unused original interpolation
constraint is deleted from the definition of legal data.

Let T be the symmetric matrix of J in (w_0,...,w_(m-1),v). For a word W,
put P_W(z)=product_(h in W)(1-hz) and S_W=sum W. The zero-clock kernels
K,T satisfy, for concatenated words U,V,

 K_UV=K_U+P_U(z)P_U(w)K_V,
 T_UV=T_U+P_U(z)P_U(w)[T_V+2S_U K_V],                         (2)
 K_[h]=h-h^2(z+w-zw)/2,
 T_[h]=h(h+1)-h^3(z+w-zw).

These follow by separating the defining time sums, retaining the offset
S_U in every second-block clock. At z=1/d_j and z=0 these compute the
actual matrix with O(m^3) scalar operations, without enumerating2^m steps.
Deletion of the last d_0 changes only T_(v,v), to S(S+1), because all
residual columns are already zero. This final diagonal is retained below.

For comparison with the earlier covariance formulation, D_i=g_i-g_(i+1),
A_ij=h_i*p_ij/d_j, beta_i=s_(i+1)(s_i+1), beta_q=S(S+1), and
c_i=(h_i-1)s_(i+1). Expansion of J and completion of the constant square give

 J=beta_q||v+sum_i beta_i D_i/beta_q||^2
       +sum_(i,l)[beta_min(i,l)-beta_i beta_l/beta_q]<D_i,D_l>
       -sum_i c_i||D_i||^2.                                    (3)

For example the coefficient of ||g_i||^2 after collecting adjacent terms
is beta_i-beta_(i-1); this proves (3) by writing g_i=v+sum_(r>=i)D_r.
Consequently positivity of T is exactly the old covariance domination,
including its constant-mode charge. This is only an equivalence of formal
quadratic forms, not a test of whether a negative formal direction is legal.

## 2. Lossless birth coordinates and exact sparsity

Define phi_k(z)=product_(r<k)(1-d_r*z),
V_k=v+sum_j phi_k(1/d_j)w_j for0<=k<=m, and U_k=V_k-v.
In particular V_m=v and U_m=0. The triangular node-evaluation matrix has
positive diagonal, so these coordinates lose no modal direction.

The identity
(1-hz)phi_k=(1-h/d_k)phi_k+(h/d_k)phi_(k+1)
gives g_i=sum_k B_ik V_k, where B starts at the point mass at0 and
at step h moves mass from state k to k+1 with fraction h/d_k.
An occupied state has d_k>=h. Lower states have already been killed by
their first physical level. Thus B_ik>=0, sum_k B_ik=1, and

 B_ik=0 if k>i or i>=2^k, for k<m.                           (4)

The first zero follows because one step advances at most one state; the
second follows by induction on first physical occurrences.

Set xi_j=d_j*w_j, z=(S+1)v, and y_k=d_k U_k. Then y=R*xi, with

 R_kj=(d_k/d_j)phi_k(1/d_j) for j>=k; R_kj=0 for j<k.

Let M=diag(1/d_0,...,1/d_(m-1),1/(S+1)) T diag(same).
Define the symmetric matrix

 H=diag(R^(-T),1) M diag(R^(-1),1)
       = [ E, ell ; ell^T, D ],       D=S/(S+1).               (5)

Thus J=sum_kl E_kl<y_k,y_l>+2sum_k ell_k<y_k,z>+D||z||^2.
All scalings are positive and R is invertible. The vectors remain in one
real Euclidean space, with their original cross terms.

There are useful EXACT zeros:

                 E_kl=0 if l>2^k and k<l<m.                  (6)

Each term in J uses only g_i and g_(i+1). If its state-k coefficient is
nonzero, (4) forces i+1<=2^k. All states at either query are then <=2^k,
so state l>2^k cannot enter that term. In particular rows0,1 have no
off-diagonal entries beyond columns1,2 respectively. Numerical arithmetic
may be replaced by zero at precisely these positions because of this proof;
no other small entries are discarded.

## 3. Exact square-factor lemma, with the complete constant load

The following is an algebraic identity for ANY symmetric real m-by-m matrix
E and real column ell, with m>=3. Define

 e_i=E_(i,i+2),                    0<=i<=m-3,
 b_i=E_(i,i+1)-2(e_(i-1)+e_i),    0<=i<=m-2,
 r_i=E_ii-E_(i-1,i)-E_(i,i+1)+e_(i-2)+e_i
                   -sum_(|k-i|>=3)|E_ik|, 0<=i<m.             (7)

An out-of-range e or adjacent entry is zero. Put alpha_(-1)=0 and

 alpha_i=ell_i-alpha_(i-1), 0<=i<m,
 C=sum_(i<m-1)alpha_i^2/b_i + alpha_(m-1)^2/r_(m-1),
 Delta=D-C.                                                   (8)

If b_i>0 and r_(m-1)>0, with no other sign assumption yet, then

 J=sum_(i<m-1)r_i||y_i||^2
     +r_(m-1)||y_(m-1)+(alpha_(m-1)/r_(m-1))*z||^2
     +sum_(i<m-1)b_i||y_i+y_(i+1)+(alpha_i/b_i)*z||^2
     +sum_(i<m-2)e_i||y_i+2y_(i+1)+y_(i+2)||^2
     +sum_(k<l, l-k>=3)|E_kl| ||y_k+sgn(E_kl)y_l||^2
     +Delta||z||^2.                                          (9)

For a zero long-range coefficient use sgn(0)=0; its whole term is zero.

Proof. The triple squares supply off-diagonal entry e_i at distance2 and
entries2e_i at each of its two neighboring pairs. Subtracting them leaves
the adjacent coefficient b_i in (7). Their diagonal contributions, together
with those of the pair squares, are
E_(i-1,i)+E_(i,i+1)-e_(i-2)-e_i. Each long-range square contributes
|E_ik| to the corresponding diagonal. The remaining diagonal is exactly r_i.
This proves the residual identity. Finally

 sum_i ell_i y_i=sum_(i<m-1)alpha_i(y_i+y_(i+1))
                                      +alpha_(m-1)y_(m-1)

by ell_i=alpha_i+alpha_(i-1). Completing precisely those displayed squares
spends C and leaves Delta. This proves (9), including all cross terms.

Therefore the explicit scalar conditions

 e_i>=0, b_i>0, r_i>=0 (all i), r_(m-1)>0, Delta>=0            (10)

are SUFFICIENT for the full T to be positive semidefinite. This supplies an
actual square-factor formula, not an LDL recurrence with unknown pivots.
The constant column is paid by the exact alternating loads (8), without a
matrix inverse or pseudoinverse. No individual adjacent-swap sign is used.

The original certificate in (1) has the additional square

 ||x_0-S*z/(S+1)-sum_j xi_j||^2.                              (11)

The change from x_0 to that square's vector is an invertible unit triangular
change of coordinates. Thus (9) plus (11) is a factorization on the FULL
original formal space, including x_0. There is no hidden range condition.

## 4. Uniform exact boundary calculation: a coefficient vanishes as 1/m

Write t=d_0, u=d_1, d=d_2. The first three gradients in birth coordinates
are V_0,V_1,V_2. Direct expansion of the first two physical steps gives

 E_00=(2-t)/t, E_01=(t-1)/u,
 E_11=[2t+u(2-u)]/u^2,
 E_12=(t+u)(u-1)/(u*d).                                     (12)

By (6), e_0=e_1=0 and both rows have no longer tails. Consequently
r_0=E_00-E_01 and r_1=E_11-E_01-E_12. Also b_0=E_01,b_1=E_12.
The actual parameters are

 t=sqrt(2)-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)],
 u=2-epsilon, d=sqrt(2)+2-lambda*epsilon^2.

For 0<epsilon<=1/128, one has 7/5<t<283/200, 199/100<u<2, and d<7/2.
The bound on t follows, for instance, from sqrt(2)>141/100 and
2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)]<5/768. Thus

 r_0>117/283-83/398>1/5,       b_0>1/5,       b_1>12/25.       (13)

For b_1, use (t+u)(u-1)> (339/100)(99/100) and
u*d<683/100, since sqrt(2)<283/200 gives d<683/200.

More significantly,

 r_1>3epsilon/2, and r_1/epsilon -> (3sqrt(2)-1)/2.            (14)

Here is a direct sign proof. Put delta=d-t-2. Then
delta/epsilon=2/[lambda(1+epsilon)]-lambda*epsilon>3/4,
using lambda<5/2 and epsilon<=1/128. Multiplication by the positive u^2*d
gives

 u^2*d*r_1
 =epsilon[(t+4)(t+2)+2-epsilon(2t+7)+epsilon^2]
       +[2+epsilon(1+t)-epsilon^2]*delta.

The bracket in the first term exceeds509/25-5/64, and the coefficient
of delta is at least2. The total therefore exceeds21epsilon, whereas
u^2*d<14. This proves the first assertion of (14). Dividing by epsilon
and taking the explicit parameter limits gives
(8+10sqrt(2))/(8+4sqrt(2))=(3sqrt(2)-1)/2.

In particular the finite observed minimum around .01267 cannot be promoted
to a uniform positive lower bound for every r_i. The factorization only
needs r_1>=0; its constant-column completion uses r_(m-1), not r_1.

The first constant loads are also exact:

 ell_0=1/(S+1),
 ell_1=[t^2/u+t+1]/(S+1),
 alpha_0=1/(S+1), alpha_1=[t^2/u+t]/(S+1).                    (15)

These follow by summing the U_0-v and U_1-v coefficients in the same two
steps. No independent scalar heights have been chosen.

## 5. Bounded computation, complete definitions, and its limits

The public ledger is research/max2_chronological_kernel_budget.json.
It records attempts BEFORE evaluation and measured cost afterward. Eight of
twelve cases used53.087538152998604 of120 measured seconds. All software was
already available: Python Decimal and a small explicit interval wrapper.
No NumPy, mpmath, SciPy, installation, optimization, or fact submission.

Cases, in order:

1. Embedded H_4 using enclosing m128 parameters, precision90: recursiveK/T
   versus direct finite sums, direct covariance identity, constant column,
   and augmented original-space matrix; calibration passed.
2. Full word m16, precision100, including the constant and augmented tests.
3. Full word m64, precision120, same tests.
4. Full word m128, precision150, same tests.
5. Embedded H_16 using enclosing m128 parameters, precision120, birth-basis
   inspection. Its residual block is an actual principal block; its constant
   diagonal is the SMALLER embedded-horizon budget, not beta_q of H_128.
   The actual full-horizon principal block adds a positive constant diagonal.
6. Full word m128, precision150, explicit local factor coefficients (7)-(8).
7. Outward intervals, embedded H_4 at enclosing m128, precision70: interval
   calibration, containment of the previous90-digit reference, and all factor
   signs passed. Exact zeros were imposed only by (6).
8. Outward intervals, full word m128, precision100: all factors in (10) passed.

The point m128 test has min residual LDL pivot .0355585548633133 and final
constant-column pivot .744470636576883 in the positive scaling preceding(5).
No pivot is used as a uniform theorem. At precision150 the smallest physical
ratio retains101 decimal digits beyond the leading one; reconstruction
residuals are at most2e-150 for T and1e-149 for the augmented matrix.

The interval run encloses each exact algebraic entry using rounded-down and
rounded-up Decimal operations. Each square root is bracketed by neighboring
representable decimals. Its certified numerical lower endpoints are

 min_i b_i > .13631289401543,
 min_i r_i > .01266933924856,
 Delta > .70074138549148,

with e_0=e_1=0 and all other e_i positive. The unrounded constant charge is
enclosed near .299258614508512. Every far entry remains in its signed square
and its absolute diagonal cost. All defining matrices, factor coefficients,
and precision data are retained in the public raw JSON outputs. This is
finite interval evidence, not acceptance by the verifier or a substitute
for the required uniform textual coefficient bounds.

Sources are retained by SHA256 in the shared research directory:
max2_chronological_kernel_b0fdefe309f1.py and
max2_chronological_kernel_interval_9114d6105f73.py. For reproduction copy
these to chronological_kernel.py and chronological_kernel_interval.py in a
working directory; the second imports the first. Execution is ledgered and
would consume another case; reading these files does not renew the allowance.

## 6. Precise remaining work

Prove (10) for the ACTUAL family uniformly over all integers m>=128, or
replace any failing coefficient bound with a different decomposition. The
new work already proves the complete identity (9), exact sparsity (6), and
the uniform first-boundary signs (13)-(15). The unresolved part is the bulk
and right-boundary coefficient control, including the scalar sum C in (8).

The strongly positive finite Delta shows why dropping the constant column
was unnecessary in this approach. The delicate r_1 limit shows why a uniform
minimum-pivot or minimum-diagonal assertion inferred from m128 would be false.
The legal negative local-swap example06fc37ec32b366f7 remains a regression;
neither the factorization nor its boundary proof assigns those swaps a sign.

Even a proof of all (10) would concern this restricted temporal quotient.
Only after that proof could (1) supply its conditional whole-quotient upper
bound. The original all-function/all-schedule target remains unresolved.

Successful call3: primal.33333333557142886, dual.333333333361537; pair violation3.96e-10, Gram minimum-4.01e-10, full dual minimum7.61e-12, flow9.99e-14, coefficient mismatch1.02e-13. # Full seven-step tangent discovery: exact dual candidate

**Status: complete joint UNVERIFIED research report.** All five authorized
jobs are consumed and the ledger is closed. There was no fact submission,
formal verification, installation, benchmark replay or subagent. The original
unrestricted gradient-descent exact-profile problem remains OPEN.

The useful output is an explicit dual over Q(sqrt(2)) for the COMPLETE
first-order cone at the frozen balanced seven-step word. The recorded exact
reconstruction satisfies all63 stationarity equations, all36 sign-price
inequalities, and an exact positive-definite LDL decomposition. It gives
the following candidate conclusion:

> Every full linearized certificate (p,Y,W) satisfies sum_i p_i <= 0.
> This conclusion covers all seven physical derivatives, all56 original
> coefficient derivatives, all36 original active signs and full R8 PSD.

This is a proof candidate with explicit algebraic evidence, not an accepted
fact. No full-neighborhood, global seven-step or original-target theorem is
claimed. In particular the discovery supplies no improving finite seed.

## 1. Inputs, exact model and scope

The active assignment is TASK.md, ONE round after completed47, completed
ceiling48/attempted49, under guidance06cd713a43322da8. The shared preceding
draft b937bb05ba1645b1 has SHA256
76a363742a60cdab32d14b828dcf7ecd58d784dfcededfd1b9510b131d67565b.
It identifies this exact cone but does not solve it. Its base uniqueness,
contact and integration claims are not imported as accepted facts here.

Accepted04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11, supplies the original full
primitive identity and composition. Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 fixes the
interpolation orientation. Accepted5e1848ee0b153f5f supplies conditional
amplification of an actual above-threshold seed only. None asserts such
a seed exists. All additional identities required below are derived jointly.

Let q=sqrt(2), l=1+q, u=q-1, and

    hbar=(q,2,q,1+l,q,2,q), Abar=l^3.

Index the eight independent formal gradient coordinates by0,...,7. Let
e be their all-ones column and let e_i be the standard coordinate columns.
For any zero-diagonal8-by8 array X, put L(X)=diag(Xe)-X. Define T0 to have
unit diagonal, entry hbar_j at i,j when j<i, and zero entries above its
diagonal. The base array has these twenty positive entries, with every
unlisted entry zero:

    01:2                  10:2l                 13:2l
    21:2lq                23:2l^2+2lq           31:2l
    32:2l^3               37:2l^2               43:2lq
    45:2l^2               47:2lq                53:4l
    54:2l^3               57:2l^3+4l            63:2lq
    65:2l^3q              67:2l^4+2l^3q+2lq
    73:2l                 75:2l^3               76:2l^5.

Call this array Lambdabar and write Lbar=L(Lambdabar). Let Z0 be the
set of the other36 ordered distinct pairs. The complete variable array
Y has Y_ii=0 and all56 other entries independently real before constraints.
For p in R7 set S=sum p_i and define

    Tp_ij=p_j for j<i, zero otherwise;
    op=(-2p_0,...,-2p_6,2S);
    D1=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar S);
    W=D1+sym(Lbar Tp+L(Y)T0), sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2;
    f=L(Y)^T e-op, n=We.

The full cone consists of f=0, Y_ij>=0 for ij in Z0 and W>=0 on R8.
The other twenty Y entries have no imposed sign. No support, rank,
reflection, equality of formal gradients or fixed shape of W is assumed.

## 2. Exact common null and lossless PSD representation

For an arbitrary word h, s=sum h_i, and full flow L^Te=(-2h,2s), put
M=D+sym(LT), D=diag(h_i(2-h_i),-s(s+2)), with T the original prefix
matrix. Since the i-th entry of Te is1+sum_(j<i)h_j,

    omega^T Te=s^2+sum h_i^2,
    e^T D e=-s^2-sum h_i^2,

so e^TMe=0. Along hbar+t p and Lambdabar+tY, the flow equation is
exact whenever f=0, and differentiation gives e^TWe=0. A symmetric PSD
matrix with zero quadratic form at e annihilates e: its quadratic form
at e+t z cannot be nonnegative for all real t unless z^TWe=0 for each z.
Thus n=0 is a necessary full-cone equation.

Let V=[I7;-1...-1], G=V^TV=I7+11^T and B=V^TWV. Its columns span
e-perp. When n=0, every vector is a sum of a multiple of e and a vector
Vz, so W>=0 on R8 if and only if B>=0 on R7. Moreover

    W>=m(I8-ee^T/8)  iff  B>=mG.

Consequently adding all8 n=0 equations and imposing this congruent PSD
condition loses no original solution. The common-null direction never
receives an epsilon perturbation. Raw readbacks contain the original
full W, every original flow equation, and the full null residual.

## 3. Both calibrations established before job1

For the singleton physical step x, choose multipliers2 and2(1+x) on the
two directed pairs. Substitution into the original two-gradient identity
gives the full remainder

    (2-x^2)(e_0-e_1)(e_0-e_1)^T.

It is nonnegative when0<=x<=q. Set x=q-t,0<=t<q. Join identical
singleton children using bridge1+u(1+x), then identical three-step
children using bridge2+x. Their respective capacities are1+x,l(1+x),
and l^2(1+x). Each balanced join has right multiplier factor l^2 and
additional interpolation factor l^2-1=2l. The accepted full join identity
therefore proves feasibility without a computation. The complete array
and its derivative are specified in frozen_model.md and in the exact
calibration artifact.

The derivative is p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1), with S=-l^2. If
d_ij=e_i-e_j, its exact remainder derivative is

    W=2q(d01 d01^T+l^2 d23 d23^T+l^2 d45 d45^T+l^4 d67 d67^T).

Every active zero-entry derivative is zero. This is a NONZERO positive
calibration from an actual feasible path. Job1 recorded exact base flow,
base M=0, derivative flow, null, all36 signs and the displayed rank-one
sum identity, then numerically solved the full fixed calibration.

For the excluded direction, v(h)=T(h)^(-1)e obeys v_0=1 and
v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L e=0,

    v^T Mv=v^TDv=1-A(h)^2 v_7^2.

At the base M=0 and Abar^2 vbar_7^2=1. Differentiating yields

    vbar^T W vbar=-2[S/l^3+l(p0+p2+p4+p6)+p1+p5+up3].

The direction p=(1,...,1) has strictly negative right side
-2(7/l^3+4l+2+u), so it cannot satisfy full PSD for any Y. This analytic
contradiction was frozen before the negative numerical calibration.

## 4. Frozen jobs and observations

Before any mathematical import or evaluation, frozen_model.md,
tangent_jobs.py and run_job.py were hashed in freeze.json. The numerical
implementation uses the already published shared Python runtime and direct
Clarabel cone data. The [official Clarabel SDP example](https://clarabel.org/stable/examples/py/example_sdp/)
specifies the upper-triangular, column-major svec convention, with q on
off-diagonal entries. The [Python interface documentation](https://clarabel.org/stable/python/getting_started_py/)
specifies the complete primal/dual return arrays. These are implementation
sources; no external mathematical theorem is used as a proof premise.

All p coordinates are bounded by |p_i|<=1 during the two discovery SDPs.
No other variable is bounded. The strict problem maximizes m subject to
S>=m, all36 Y_zero>=m, f=n=0 and B>=mG. If the frozen credibility test
fails, the nonstrict problem maximizes S subject to the complete cone.
All options, thresholds and reconstruction choices are in the freeze.

The job3 numerical gap was2.547742288815272e-9; its smallest recorded
complement eigenvalue was-1.420580825145002e-9. It does not supply a
strict tangent. Job4's numerical gap was7.87119183312904e-8; its original

All five jobs returned process code0; no timeout or repair occurred.
`AlmostSolved` is a reduced-accuracy status, preserved without relabeling.
The positive calibration's acceptance follows the frozen tolerance rule

Each job was charged the FULL8 seconds before launch and externally

All original primal and dual arrays, dense conic data, index maps,

## 5. The exact dual and the candidate proof

Job5 rounded free coordinates once to multiples of2^-20 and solved exact
affine equations in the field Q(q), q^2=2. All p-box prices were set to
zero as a reconstruction ansatz. This was not a restriction of either SDP.
No optimization or parameter grid was used in reconstruction.

The appendix specifies two eight-coordinate vectors a,b (the first and
last halves of `equality_prices`), a symmetric7-by7 matrix Z through its
28 svec entries, and36 coefficients sigma_ij. All their displayed numbers
are exact rational pairs (r,s), meaning r+s q. Off-diagonal svec entries
are q Z_ij, and diagonal entries are Z_ii.

The algebraic identity certified by the63 zero stationarity residuals is

    -S = trace(Z B)+sum_(ij in Z0) sigma_ij Y_ij
         -a^T f-b^T n.                                      (D)

This is an identity for EVERY p and EVERY zero-diagonal real Y, before
any cone constraint. There is no p-normalization term because all14 box
prices are exactly zero.

Here is an explicit way to check every coefficient of(D) without a
solver. Set H=VZV^T and K=H-sym(b e^T). Write t_i for the transpose of
the i-th row of T0 and r_ij=t_i-t_j. A single unit Y_ij contributes
e_i-e_j to f and sym(e_i r_ij^T) to W. Thus the56 required identities are

    a_i-a_j-(K r_ij)_i = 0              on the20 base-positive pairs,
    a_i-a_j-(K r_ij)_i = sigma_ij       on the36 other pairs. (Y)

For each i<7 let C_i have entry1 in column i strictly below its diagonal,
and zero elsewhere. The unit p_i contribution to W is

    N_i=2(1-hbar_i)e_i e_i^T-2Abar e_7e_7^T+sym(Lbar C_i).

Its contribution to f is2e_i-2e_7. The seven remaining identities are

    2(a_i-a_7)-trace(K N_i)=1, i=0,...,6.                  (P)

The appendixed numbers and q^2=2 specify all entries in(Y) and(P).
The reconstruction artifact records each of their63 residuals as the
exact pair(0,0), using the equivalent unsimplified original coefficient
map. It retains the exact source and deterministic arithmetic procedure.

All36 sigma values are nonnegative in the real embedding q>0, and none
is zero. The following elementary rule makes their comparisons exact:
if r,s have the same sign, use that sign; if they have opposite signs,
the sign of r+s q is sign(r) times sign(r^2-2s^2). The cases r=0 or
s=0 are immediate. The nonzero opposite-sign case cannot yield zero,
because q is irrational. This is the precise rule used by the frozen
exact arithmetic, with rational operations only.

For Z, the artifact supplies Z=L diag(d_0,...,d_6)L^T with unit lower
triangular L and all seven pivots d_i strictly positive under that same
sign rule. The appendix includes L and all seven exact pairs. The LDL
recurrence is

    d_k=Z_kk-sum_(j<k)L_kj^2 d_j,
    L_ik=(Z_ik-sum_(j<k)L_ij L_kj d_j)/d_k, i>k.

These formulas prove that the supplied decomposition entails Z>0, with
no floating eigenvalue inference. All pivots were tested exactly before
division, and a zero pivot with nonzero remaining column would have been
rejected. The recorded result has no zero pivot.

For a full feasible tangent, f=n=0, every Y_ij in Z0 is nonnegative and
B>=0. If Z=L diag(d)L^T, then
trace(ZB)=sum_k d_k (L e_k)^T B(L e_k)>=0. Applying(D) gives -S>=0.
This proves the candidate first-order capacity bound from the explicit
algebraic checks. A zero tangent attains S=0, so the normalized nonstrict
problem has exact optimum0 at draft level. The strict model likewise has
optimum0: S>=m and S<=0 imply m<=0, and the zero point with m=0 is feasible.

The same identity also gives a useful equality condition: if S=0, each
active Y entry is zero, and B=0 because Z is positive definite. To see
the latter without a spectral theorem, each nonnegative summand
(L e_k)^T B(L e_k) must vanish; PSD then makes B annihilate the basis
formed by the columns of L. Consequently W=0 as well. This does not
by itself settle how a finite higher-order perturbation can behave.

## 6. What this closes and what remains

At candidate level this resolves the specific missing lifted tangent
interface in b937bb05ba1645b1: its contact-surviving vector
p_test=(0,0,0,0,0,2,-1), with S=1, admits NO full first-order completion
Y. The obstruction is independent of the attempted W_test, any skew
parameterization or any support choice. It applies to all W and Y in
the original linearized cone.

The proof does not establish a neighborhood capacity bound. The exact
finite remainder still contains

    D2(p)+sym(L(Y)Tp),
    D2(p)=diag(-p_0^2,...,-p_6^2,-S^2).

A finite feasible perturbation may have zero first-order capacity change,
singular leading terms, or coefficient difference quotients not known
to be bounded. A separate argument must control these terms and all
nearby full certificates. The reconstructed equality condition above
is a concrete input for such a future argument, after verification.
The reconstruction also records affine elimination rank27; this is
preserved as finite algebraic telemetry, not promoted here to an
additional coercivity or neighborhood theorem.

No full strict positive-capacity tangent was found, so the sufficient
integration construction was not invoked and no finite seed parameter
was selected. A small numerical residual or an infeasibility label is
never used as a mathematical conclusion. The original target is still
OPEN, and the only deliverable eligible for a future formal gate is the
joint algebraic tangent theorem with all its explicit checks.

## 7. Persistence and literature process

Its canonical ledger is the adjacent full_tangent_discovery_20261001_budget.json.

readback, exact certificate and log. Neither a hash nor a successful
program exit is a verifier acceptance. Shared-memory evidence will include
this entire manuscript and its exact appendix, rather than a private path.

The required Matlas search for PSD tangent/strict-complement perturbation
returned copositive-tensor and distributed-system statements with different
objects and hypotheses. Neither was used. The existing source's perturbation
background was already recalled. Official Clarabel documentation was read
for the solver encoding only. All mathematics used in this report is either
in the named accepted predecessor or is explicitly part of this joint
unverified derivation. There is no literature-based seed or local-optimality
claim. No new evaluation is permitted after the closed ledger.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001101 -->
# Two retained signed modes and a long step: complete scalar dominance

Status: UNVERIFIED joint proof draft, max2 round29, first of the TWO
analytical rounds allocated after completed baseline28 by TASK.md and
master_guidance4226374d0649bf55. No formal-verifier allocation is open.
No numerical evaluation, scan, solver, machine algebra, replay,
installation, consult, or subagent was used. Nothing below is a new fact.

Accepted inputs:1252b8823bb5a290, proof paragraphs1--2, for the ordered
smooth interpolation convention;04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11, for
the original primitive identity and primitive composition identity.
Every additional ingredient is proved jointly below. No earlier
annihilation or retained-terminal draft is used as an accepted premise.

This is a complete dominance result for the explicit domain below,
including all joint values, amplitudes, and curvatures. Both modes are
retained and signed on an open domain and the second physical step is
strictly greater than2. There is a genuine positive joint value price.
That price adds no negative primitive-energy test beyond the COMPLETE
value-optimized scalar channel. A full original certificate is also
given for one below-threshold member, to prove the domain is not
already excluded by the older necessary conditions.

This does not give a threshold seed, prove a global no-seed theorem, or
decide the original exact-profile Goal. The goal's convention stays
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-min f)/(L||x_0-x_star||^2), hence W=2R.

Notation: all new local letters a,b,c,d,alpha,beta,u,v,p_i,z_i,ell,m,
t,U_alpha,U_beta,e_i^theta,d_i^theta,r_i^theta,J_theta,X_i,Y_i,
E_tail,E_mix,j_min,Conj,A_i,MinHull,R_true,kappa,delta in this draft
mean their versions with suffix29. They do not redefine project
symbols with the same printed names. The schedule h, mass s, original
Q_ij, Lambda_ij, L_flow, T, D_prim and M_prim retain their stated roles.

## 1. Actual word, curvatures, amplitudes, and all signs

Fix a word in the domain

    h=(a,b,c,d),
    7/5<=a<=7/4,   2<b<=9/4,   0<=c,d<=a.                 (1)

Set s=a+b+c+d and C=1+s. These are variable, not a fixed-capacity
slice. Define

    beta_max=min({1} union {1/c:c>0} union {1/d:d>0}),
    1/b<=alpha<=1/a,      1/a<=beta<=beta_max,
    u,v>=0.                                               (2)

The beta interval is nonempty because c,d<=a and a>1. Negative initial
amplitudes give equivalent geometries by coordinate reflection, so
nonnegative u,v loses nothing. Both pure-amplitude endpoints are retained.

For i=0,...,4 define

    p_i=product_(0<=j<i)(1-alpha*h_j),
    z_i=product_(0<=j<i)(1-beta*h_j),
    x_i=(u*p_i,v*z_i),
    g_i=(alpha*u*p_i,beta*v*z_i).                          (3)

The empty products are1. These are the exact samples of ONE static,
convex, 1-smooth base function

    F_base(x,y)=alpha*x^2/2+beta*y^2/2.

The gradient-descent recursion is x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i. All original
indices, including the terminal index4, are present.

Put ell=1-a*alpha and m=ell*(b*alpha-1). Then

    p=(1,ell,-m,-m*(1-c*alpha),
                         -m*(1-c*alpha)*(1-d*alpha)).      (4)

Thus ell,m>=0. Both tail factors lie in[0,1]. The fast samples obey

    z_1<=0,  z_2=(a*beta-1)*(b*beta-1)>=0,
    0<=z_4<=z_3<=z_2<=15/16<1.                            (5)

For the last bound, (a*beta-1)*(b*beta-1) increases for beta>=1/a:
its derivative is2ab*beta-a-b>=b-a>0. Hence it is at most
(a-1)*(b-1)<=(3/4)*(5/4)=15/16. The two beta-tail factors are
nonnegative by(2).

On the open domain with strict curvature endpoints, 0<c,d<1/beta,
and u,v>0, the slow signs are +,+,-,-,- and the fast signs are
+,-,+,+,+. Both modes survive to index4; neither is an early-killed
mode. This open set is nonempty even for the full-certificate word in
Section9. Curvature endpoints, c=0, d=0, and all product-zero cases
are included in(1)--(5). The limiting face b=2 obeys the same formulas,
although the selected family itself has b>2.

## 2. The original primitive interface and exact terminal-source price

For distinct i,j in{0,1,2,3,4}, use

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

All TWENTY ordered constraints Q_ij<=0 are imposed. The primitive
energy is exactly

    V=2sum_(i<4)h_i(f_i-f_4)
             +sum_(i<4)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-sC||g_4||^2.   (6)

For the original full certificate retain all twenty Lambda_ij>=0,
zero diagonal, and define

    e=(1,1,1,1,1)^T,  L_flow=diag(Lambda*e)-Lambda,
    omega=(-2a,-2b,-2c,-2d,2s)^T,
    T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
    D_prim=diag(a(2-a),b(2-b),c(2-c),d(2-d),-s(s+2)).

Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the exact identity

    L_flow^T e=omega,
    M_prim=D_prim+(L_flow*T+T^T*L_flow^T)/2,
    V+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
                          =<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_4)>.    (7)

A seed still requires M_prim positive semidefinite on ALL of R^5.
No coordinate, sign, pair, or zero-step formal gradient is deleted.

Write U_theta=theta*(1-theta)/2 for theta=alpha,beta. Substitution in
Q_ij at the base values gives the symmetric nonnegative slack

    w_ij=-Q_ij(F_base)
         =u^2 U_alpha(p_i-p_j)^2+v^2 U_beta(z_i-z_j)^2.    (8)

For arbitrary new values f_i=F_base(x_i)+epsilon_i the complete
constraint system is epsilon_i-epsilon_j<=w_ij. Let D_i be the
shortest w-cost from the ORIGINAL terminal source4 to node i in the
complete five-node graph, with D_4=0. A shortest simple path exists:
weights are nonnegative and cycles can be removed, including zero-cost
cycles. Following a path from4 to i gives epsilon_i-epsilon_4>=-D_i.
All h_i are nonnegative, so the simultaneous all-value minimum is

    V_min=V_base-2sum_(i<4)h_i D_i.                       (9)

It is attained by epsilon_i=-D_i. Indeed the distance triangle
inequality D_j<=D_i+w_ij proves every ordered residual is nonpositive.

The matching auxiliary flow sends2h_i units from4 to each i<4 along
an attaining path. Conversely w_ij>=D_j-D_i and(7)'s flow give
sum Lambda_ij w_ij>=2sum h_i D_i. This proves the exact price and its
terminal-source orientation. An optimal auxiliary price flow is not
asserted to be a full PSD certificate.

## 3. Scalar distances and energies: explicit formulas

For real points t_i on a line, the shortest path with squared-distance
edge weights from one sampled value to another consists of all sampled
values between them in monotone order, with ties allowed. Here is a
proof that also covers endpoints and equal values. List the distinct
ordered sampled values and their nonnegative adjacent gaps. The square
of any edge spanning several gaps is at least the sum of the squared
gaps it spans. Every path between the selected endpoints crosses every
gap between them at least once. Its cost is therefore at least the
sum of those squared gaps; the monotone path attains that sum. Excursions
outside the interval cannot improve it. Tied nodes add zero gaps and
remain valid original nodes.

For q_i=p_i or z_i define

    e_1(q)=(q_1-q_4)^2,
    e_2(q)=(q_2-q_3)^2+(q_3-q_4)^2,
    e_3(q)=(q_3-q_4)^2,
    e_4(q)=0.                                           (10)

For the slow coordinate, the complete order is

    p_2<=p_3<=p_4<=p_1<=p_0=1.

For the fast coordinate, it is

    z_1<=z_4<=z_3<=z_2<z_0=1.

Consequently the unweighted scalar distances from terminal4 are

    d_0^alpha=(1-p_1)^2+(p_1-p_4)^2,
    d_0^beta =(1-z_2)^2+(z_2-z_3)^2+(z_3-z_4)^2,
    d_i^alpha=e_i(p), d_i^beta=e_i(z),  i=1,2,3,4.         (11)

Define the base scalar energy for any q_i=product_(j<i)(1-theta*h_j)
by direct substitution into(6):

    B_theta=theta sum_(i<4)h_i[1+theta(1-h_i)]q_i^2
                      -s*theta[1+C*theta]q_4^2.          (12)

The exact all-value scalar minimum at that curvature and unit initial
amplitude is

    J_theta=B_theta-theta(1-theta)sum_(i<4)h_i d_i^theta,
                         theta=alpha or beta.            (13)

Equations(9)--(11), or the one-dimensional conjugate in Section8,
prove that this is attained by an actual scalar smooth convex function
with a minimizer. It is not just the energy of the original quadratic:
ALL scalar values have been optimized using ALL original pairs.

## 4. Exact joint distances and the four competing prices

For the four nodes1,4,3,2, both coordinates have the same monotone
node order, with opposite coordinate directions. The path along this
chain between any pair simultaneously attains both scalar shortest
distances. Node0 is outside the coordinate range of this chain in each
coordinate, so it cannot lower either scalar distance. Thus the joint
distances to i=1,2,3 are exactly

    D_i=u^2 U_alpha e_i(p)+v^2 U_beta e_i(z).              (14)

A shortest simple path to0 has a last chain node k in{1,4,3,2}.
Its prefix can be replaced by a shortest chain path, so exactly

    D_0=min_(k in{1,4,3,2})
       [u^2 U_alpha r_k(p)+v^2 U_beta r_k(z)],
    r_k(q)=e_k(q)+(1-q_k)^2.                             (15)

This argument examines every possible path; it does not assume that a
chronological path must remain shortest after a sign change.

Define the ENERGY price increments

    X_k=2a U_alpha[r_k(p)-d_0^alpha],
    Y_k=2a U_beta [r_k(z)-d_0^beta].                      (16)

They already include the original sink0 demand2a. All are nonnegative,
X_1=0, Y_2=0. Expanding differences of squares gives

    X_4=4a U_alpha(1-p_1)(p_1-p_4),
    X_3=X_4+4a U_alpha(p_4-p_3)(1-p_3),
    X_2=X_3+4a U_alpha(p_3-p_2)(1-p_2),

    Y_3=4a U_beta(z_2-z_3)(1-z_2),
    Y_4=Y_3+4a U_beta(z_3-z_4)(1-z_3),
    Y_1=Y_4+4a U_beta(z_4-z_1)(1-z_1).                   (17)

Hence0=X_1<=X_4<=X_3<=X_2 and0=Y_2<=Y_3<=Y_4<=Y_1.
The EXACT full all-value answer at all amplitudes and curvatures is

    V_min=u^2 J_alpha+v^2 J_beta
                  -min_(k in{1,4,3,2})(u^2 X_k+v^2 Y_k). (18)

On the strict open domain from Section1 with beta<1 and c,d>0, all
four candidates in the final minimum are positive for u,v>0. Thus the
joint price is strictly larger than the sum of the separate scalar
prices. The theorem below pays this actual positive price; it does not
pretend the coordinatewise minimizations are additive.

There is also an exact finite amplitude elimination. Normalize
u^2+v^2=1 and put lambda=u^2. For fixed alpha,beta the right side is
the maximum of the four affine lines

    L_k(lambda)=A_k+B_k lambda,
    A_k=J_beta-Y_k,
    B_k=(J_alpha-X_k)-(J_beta-Y_k).                       (19)

Its minimum on[0,1] is attained at0,1, or a pairwise intersection
lambda=(A_l-A_k)/(B_k-B_l) lying in[0,1], for B_k!=B_l. Evaluate
the maximum of ALL four lines at each candidate. Parallel or identical
lines create no missing candidate: a flat minimum interval has an
endpoint at an intersection or at0 or1. At most six intersections
and the two endpoints suffice. This covers amplitude-zero boundaries,
ties, and collapsed branches without a numerical optimization.

## 5. The retained slow mode pays the full price through node2

The quantitative estimate is

                 J_alpha-X_2>=41 alpha^2/576.             (20)

This is uniform over every word and curvature in(1)--(2), independent
of the other mode. The proof is given completely here.

First observe from(16) that J_alpha-X_2 is the scalar energy obtained
by using the route4->3->2->0 for sink0, the direct route4->1 for
sink1, and the usual monotone routes for sinks2,3. This route choice
is an UPPER bound on the scalar price, rather than its optimum.
Call the corresponding algebraic energy E_tail. Its value is allowed
to be below the scalar minimum; we prove even E_tail is positive.

Use theta=alpha, ell=1-a*theta, m=ell*(b*theta-1). After the first
two steps the scalar point is -m. Append a list of zero, one, or two
tail steps, each between0 and a. Let T_tail be its sum, s_tail=a+b+T_tail,
and let -t be its terminal point. For a tail index k let p_k be its
point just before that update, and T_k the tail sum up to and including
that update. Then direct telescoping gives

 E_tail=1-(1+s_tail*theta)^2 t^2
       -2theta(1-theta)[a(1+m)+b*ell*(ell+t)
                    +theta sum_(tail k)(a+T_k)h_k p_k^2]. (21)

Here is a derivation retaining every term. For any scalar sampled
values write f_i=x_i*g_i-g_i^2/2-A_i. Substitution in(6), followed by
x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i, gives

    V=x_0^2-(x_end+s_tail*g_end)^2
                                      -2sum h_i(A_i-A_end).

For the specified route prices A_i=U_theta(p_i^2+d_i), A_end=U_theta*t^2.
For a negative monotone tail,

    p_i^2-t^2+d_i=2theta sum_(k>=i in tail)h_k p_k^2.

For index0 the same expression is2(1+m) plus the expression for
index2; for index1 it is2ell(ell+t). Collecting each tail update's
coefficient gives a+T_k. As2U_theta=theta(1-theta), these identities
prove(21), with no restriction on zero tail steps.

Now append one more tail step r in[0,a] at the old terminal point -y,
where y>=0, and write S for the old total mass. The new terminal
magnitude is y(1-r*theta). Subtracting(21) before and after the step
gives the EXACT increment

 E_new-E_old
  =theta^2*r*y^2{
      theta(S+r)[2(S+1)-theta*r*(S+r)]+2b(1-theta)}
       +2b*ell*y*theta^2*r*(1-theta).                    (22)

To check the terminal-square part, use
(1+(S+r)theta)(1-r theta)=1+S theta-r(S+r)theta^2.
The new summand in(21) is theta(S-b+r)r*y^2, and its b*ell term
changes by -b*ell*y*r*theta. These give exactly(22).

All terms in(22) are nonnegative. Indeed theta*r<=1, and

    2(S+1)-theta*r*(S+r)>=S+2-r>0,

because S>=a+b and r<=a. Also theta<1, ell,y,r>=0. Thus
E_tail is bounded below by the corresponding two-step energy E_empty.
This is a proved energy increment, not an assumed prefix or suffix
heredity property of primitive schedules.

For the two-step word(a,b), the routes in question are both direct
from its terminal point -m. Expanding their two ordered interpolation
residuals, or setting T_tail=0 in(21), gives

 E_empty/theta^2
  =a(2-a)-b(b-2)ell^2
       -[2(1-theta)/theta^2]m
       -[2(1-theta)/theta^2+2(a+b)/theta+(a+b)^2]m^2.      (23)

All estimates for(23) follow from the explicit parameter box, without
any older necessary inequality. They are

    (1-theta)/theta^2<=b(b-1)<=45/16,
    a+b<=4,
    2(1-theta)/theta^2+2(a+b)/theta+(a+b)^2
                                           <=317/8<40,
    ell<=1-a/b,
    0<=m=(1-a theta)(b theta-1)<=(b-a)^2/(4ab).            (24)

The first bound uses theta>=1/b and the decreasing function
(1-theta)/theta^2 on(0,1]. The last bound follows by completing the
square in the concave quadratic in theta. Its right side increases
with b/a>=1.

If7/5<=a<=3/2, then

    a(2-a)>=3/4, ell<=17/45<2/5,
    m<=289/5040<1/16, b(b-2)<=9/16.

Therefore(23) is at least

    3/4-9/100-45/128-5/32=487/3200>41/576.               (25)

If3/2<=a<=7/4, then

    a(2-a)>=7/16, ell<=1/3, m<=1/24.

Equation(23) is now at least

    7/16-1/16-15/64-5/72=41/576.                         (26)

Both ranges include their common boundary. Equations(22),(25),(26)
prove(20) jointly, including m=0, ell=0, and terminal product zero.

## 6. Full amplitude/curvature comparison with the complete scalar channel

The k=2 route in(18) has Y_2=0. Thus(18) and(20) imply the uniform
pointwise inequality

    V_min>=u^2(J_alpha-X_2)+v^2 J_beta
            >=(41/576)alpha^2*u^2+v^2 J_beta.             (27)

Let E_mix(h) be the infimum of(18) over all alpha,beta in(2) and
u^2+v^2=1. The infimum is attained: the parameter intervals and the
amplitude segment are compact and(18) is continuous. Define

    j_min(h)=min_(1/a<=beta<=beta_max) J_beta(h).

Pure fast amplitudes u=0,v=1 attain J_beta. Equation(27) proves

    min(0,E_mix(h))=min(0,j_min(h)),
    E_mix(h)>=min(41/(576b^2),j_min(h)).                  (28)

Indeed if j_min<0, every mixture has value at least j_min and the
pure fast minimizer attains it. If j_min>=0 every mixture is
nonnegative. This eliminates ALL slow curvatures and ALL amplitudes
for the complete sign comparison, not merely one selected ratio.

There is no unresolved fast-curvature sign branch. Formula(13), with
the beta distances in(11), is a single explicit polynomial in beta
of degree at most10 on the entire interval[1/a,beta_max]. Thus
j_min is obtained exactly from the two endpoints and every real root
of its derivative in the interval. If the derivative is identically
zero, either endpoint gives the constant value. Equal endpoints and
multiple roots cause no omission. No polynomial root has been
numerically estimated here; this is an exact finite algebraic rule.

For precision about the older system, denote by S_scalar(h) the
COMPLETE value-optimized scalar channel: for every theta in[0,1],
the scalar samples q_i=product_(j<i)(1-theta h_j), gradients theta*q_i,
and every jointly interpolable choice of their values must have V>=0.
This is the scalar channel used for the retained-mode comparison;
bare quadratic values alone are a strictly different test definition.
Only its inequalities J_beta>=0 for beta in(2) are used here.

Let N_old(h) denote the conjunction of ANY other old cut, triangle,
return, curvature-repair, compact-box, or full-cone necessary tests,
with their whole original parameter domains retained. Then(28) gives
the exact logical comparison

 [N_old(h) and S_scalar(h) and nonnegativity of this entire joint family]
                iff [N_old(h) and S_scalar(h)]             (29)

throughout(1). No old test is discarded or replaced. None of N_old is
needed to prove this implication. The only old necessary conditions
actually USED are the displayed fully optimized scalar inequalities.
The bounds in(1) are explicit restrictions of the investigated family,
not claimed new necessary conditions on every four-step seed.

The distinction matters: this draft does NOT claim that a smaller
finite list using only unrepriced quadratics, cut tests, and C83 implies
J_beta>=0. No independence from that smaller list has been tested or
asserted. The proved statement is complete dominance by actual scalar
value-optimized witnesses, hence redundancy after the complete scalar
channel even when every other old condition is retained.

If this joint family supplies any negative-energy witness, (28) supplies
a pure scalar witness with at least as negative an energy under the
same amplitude normalization. This is an energy comparison; it is not
a comparison of normalized terminal objective payoffs after repricing.

## 7. Boundaries and the original formal space

No zero-step compression is used. If c=0 or d=0 the corresponding
points and gradients coincide, the intervening costs vanish, and both
directed inequalities force equality of values. The original nodes
and all twenty multipliers nevertheless remain in(7)--(18).

At alpha=1/b the slow mode dies at index2; at alpha=1/a it dies at
index1. At beta=1/a the fast mode dies at index1; at beta=1/c or1/d,
when present, it dies at that tail update. Equations(4)--(28) retain
every such case. These boundaries do not change the fact that BOTH
modes are genuinely retained on the open domain in Section1.

If beta=1 then U_beta=0 and all Y_k vanish, so k=1 gives zero joint
price. If one amplitude vanishes, (18) reduces to the corresponding
scalar optimum. Tied distances, tied prices, and equal curvature
alpha=beta=1/a are included. No strict ordering is used in an endpoint
proof, and no sign is divided out.

## 8. One static attaining function, actual minimizers, and true distance

Take the jointly minimizing values f_i=F_base(x_i)-D_i. On R^2 set

    cjet_i=<g_i,x_i>-f_i,
    Conj_i(w)=cjet_i+<x_i,w-g_i>+||w-g_i||^2/2,
    Conj(w)=max_(0<=i<=4)Conj_i(w),
    F(x)=sup_w[<x,w>-Conj(w)].                            (30)

This construction uses every finite node, including terminal4, and
does not force the base origin to remain a minimizer.

For a self-contained verification, Conj is finite, 1-strongly convex,
and has quadratic growth. The supremum defining F has a unique
maximizer w(x). Its two strong-convexity inequalities imply

    <x-x',w(x)-w(x')> >= ||w(x)-w(x')||^2.

Thus w is1-Lipschitz. Comparing the defining maxima at two nearby
points shows differentiability with gradient w: the difference between
F(x+d)-F(x) and <w(x),d> is bounded in absolute value by ||d||^2.
The supremum representation gives convexity and finiteness.

Expanding at g_i gives Conj_j(g_i)-cjet_i=Q_ij<=0. Equality holds
for j=i, so Conj(g_i)=cjet_i and x_i is a subgradient of Conj at g_i.
Consequently F(x_i)=f_i and grad F(x_i)=g_i. This proves actual static
attainment of all the joint values and the entire original GD word.

Define

    A_i=Conj_i(0)=D_i-u^2 U_alpha p_i^2-v^2 U_beta z_i^2,
    A_max=max_i A_i,  Active={i:A_i=A_max},
    y_i=x_i-g_i=((1-alpha)u*p_i,(1-beta)v*z_i).

Then the true minimizers and minimum value are

    MinHull=argmin F=conv{y_i:i in Active},
    min F=-A_max.                                        (31)

To prove this, the directional derivative of Conj at0 is
max_(i in Active)<y_i,w>. Its subgradient set is the displayed convex
hull: the hull gives supporting inequalities, and strict separation
rules out a vector outside the hull. By the unique-maximizer condition,
grad F(x)=0 iff x belongs to that subgradient set. This proves(31).

For u^2+v^2>0, x_0 lies outside MinHull. Equations(4)--(5) imply
p_i,z_i<=1 for every i, so

    <x_0,x_0-y_i>
      =u^2[1-(1-alpha)p_i]+v^2[1-(1-beta)z_i]
      >=alpha*u^2+beta*v^2>0.                            (32)

Hence R_true=dist(x_0,MinHull)>0. An exact finite formula for R_true^2
is the minimum of the distances to every active vertex and every
segment between active vertices. For distinct y_i,y_j use

    tau_ij=min(1,max(0,<x_0-y_i,y_j-y_i>/||y_j-y_i||^2)),
    distance_ij^2=||x_0-y_i-tau_ij(y_j-y_i)||^2.           (33)

Coincident vertices use their vertex candidate. An exterior point's
closest point in a planar convex hull lies on a vertex or edge, and
every listed segment lies in that hull; this proves equality.

Since D_4=0, the TRUE W-normalized terminal objective of this actual
attaining function is

    2[F_base(x_4)+A_max]/R_true^2.                        (34)

Translate a closest minimizer to0, subtract min F, and rescale by
R_true to obtain an initial distance1 instance with exactly this
payoff and the same word. Primitive energy changes only by the square
of the length scale. Formula(28) does not claim a payoff comparison
between the mixed and pure witnesses with their different minimizer
sets. This prevents the fixed-origin error identified in the prior wave.

## 9. A full-certificate member surviving EVERY old necessary test

This section proves the word domain is not one already excluded by
the complete older system. It does not assert a threshold survivor.

Let a=sqrt(2), let delta be the unique positive solution of

    delta(4+a+delta)=3(1+a),

and set h=(a,1+delta,1,1). The increasing left side is smaller than
the right at delta=1, since its difference there is2-2a<0. At
delta=5/4 the difference is(57-28a)/16>0, since a<3/2.
Therefore1<delta<5/4, and this word lies in(1). Its two retained
curvature intervals have nonempty interiors.

Set

    T_mass=4+a+delta, kappa=(T_mass/3)^2>1.

Use the following original finite multipliers, with every other
off-diagonal entry zero and every diagonal entry zero:

    Lambda_01=2,           Lambda_10=2a+2,
    Lambda_14=delta(kappa-1),
    Lambda_21=kappa-1,     Lambda_23=2kappa,
    Lambda_24=kappa-1,
    Lambda_31=kappa-1,     Lambda_32=4kappa,
    Lambda_34=9kappa-1,
    Lambda_41=kappa-1,     Lambda_43=12kappa.              (35)

All twenty signs are satisfied. The exact full formal identity is

    V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij
        =kappa[||g_2-g_3||^2+4||g_3-g_4||^2].            (36)

Here is a derivation from the accepted composition identity, including
its child certificates. For a one-step word a=sqrt(2), direct expansion
gives

    V_(a)+2 Q_01+(2a+2)Q_10=0.                           (37)

For the two unit steps, temporarily on local indices0,1,2, direct
substitution gives

    V_(1,1)+2Q_10+4Q_21=||g_0||^2+3||g_1||^2-4||g_2||^2.

For a unit update Q_ij+Q_ji, with j=i+1, equals
||g_j||^2-<g_i,g_j>. Adding2(Q_01+Q_10)+8(Q_12+Q_21) therefore
turns the right side into ||g_0-g_1||^2+4||g_1-g_2||^2. Thus the
two-step child multipliers are Lambda_01=2,Lambda_10=4,
Lambda_12=8,Lambda_21=12, with the stated full PSD remainder.

The accepted04219554e41d9750 Section11 primitive join has child
capacities1+a and3, bridge excess delta, and scale kappa. Its exact
added interpolation combination is

    (kappa-1)[Q_21+Q_31+Q_41+delta Q_14+Q_24+Q_34].

Embedding the two proved child identities on the original nodes
0,1 and2,3,4 gives exactly(35)--(36). For a flow audit, the join
identity (kappa-1)(3-delta)=2(1+a+delta) gives net flows
(-2a,-2(1+delta),-2,-2,2(a+3+delta)), exactly omega.
The remainder in(36) is PSD on all five independent formal gradients;
no quotient or rank restriction was imposed.

Consequently every actual trajectory on this word has nonnegative
primitive energy. Every valid old necessary test, with all its
parameters, passes; otherwise its negative actual witness would
contradict(36). This is stronger than checking a subset of old tests.
The same word still has a strictly positive joint price for the
interior curvatures and amplitudes described in Section4.

Its capacity is T_mass<4+3/2+5/4=27/4<193/25, so(35) is expressly
NOT the missing threshold seed. The shared result remains an
unverified joint draft rather than an accepted new fact.

## 10. Structural closure and the genuinely unclosed next regime

The two retained signed modes in(1)--(2) do not produce a new negative
test after the complete optimized scalar channel. The structural reason
is precise: nodes1,4,3,2 admit a common monotone order, so only sink0
has an incompatible order; routing that sink through2 costs at most
the slow mode's positive budget. The surviving negative tail of the
slow mode does not break the argument because its exact increment(22)
is nonnegative. This is not an annihilation argument.

Do not repeat this family by changing amplitudes, scalar curvature
endpoints, positive or zero tail steps within(2), or choosing another
value path. Equations(18)--(29) already cover all of them.

The nearest unclosed regime, suitable for the SECOND allocated round,
keeps the same genuine long-step word box and the same retained slow
curvature, but allows c*beta>1 or d*beta>1. A fast tail reversal can
destroy the common order of nodes1,4,3,2 and create more than one
conflicting sink. The single-sink proof above does not apply there.
Both tails reversing, with fast signs+,-,+,-,+ and slow signs
+,+,-,-,-, is a concrete such interior. This is not a new allocation
or an assertion that the remaining regime yields an obstruction.

Also unclosed are other long-step boxes, other curvature placements,
different static bases, the full variable-h/twenty-multiplier/ambient-R^5
threshold decision, and the original unrestricted exact-profile Goal.

## 11. Retrieval and accounting

Read TASK.md, latest master_guidance4226374d0649bf55, the accepted
facts and glossary, prior local handoff28, and shared findings/dead ends
including26ce61f19dc060a7,5ffb49642281af3a and8fef2e0da3480da1.
The fact search found no accepted result already establishing this
two-retained long-step comparison.

Matlas search on two-eigenvalue GD value optimization returned unrelated
theorems. Web retrieval located the known smooth convex interpolation
paper arXiv:1502.05666. Its exact local text
downloads/1502.05666.txt, Theorem4 and Remark2 with their proof, was
read. It supplied context, not an external black-box premise: every
construction used here was reproved from the accepted project interface.
No external theorem or new fact is claimed from this retrieval.

Resources used: ZERO numerical evaluations, solvers, symbolic-algebra
jobs, replay, formal verification, installation, consults, or subagents.
File writing and hashing are operational only. This is the first of
two assigned analytical rounds after baseline28, not a renewal of any
earlier ledger and not a claim about runtime termination.

# A quantitative final-stage guard for every nonnegative prefix

Status: complete candidate proof, NOT verifier-accepted. No new submission is
authorized. This proof constructs and analyzes the function directly. It does
not use the unverified minimax finding 40601f4c6f496ab8 as a premise. The final
maximal-extension identification uses only accepted fact 1252b8823bb5a290.

## 1. Statement in the original normalization

Fix any integer k>=0, any L>0, and any finite nonnegative predetermined prefix
h_pre=(h_0,...,h_(k-1)). The empty prefix is allowed. Write S=sum_t h_t. Define

    t_S=(sqrt(8S+9)-1)/4,
    b_S=1+t_S=(3+sqrt(8S+9))/4,
    c_S=1/(S+1),
    q_S=1/(2t_S+1)^2=1/[1+2(S+b_S)],
    omega_S=1/(1+q_S t_S),
    kappa_S=q_S^2/[t_S(1+q_S t_S)].                      (1)

All these quantities are positive, 0<omega_S<1, and t_S>=1/2. The identities

    t_S(2t_S+1)=S+1,
    c_S^2 t_S^2=q_S                                   (2)

will be used repeatedly.

There is an explicit SINGLE convex differentiable L-smooth function f on R^2,
with a minimizer x_star=0 and an initial point x_0 of norm one, such that for
EVERY nonnegative final stepsize b, the GD trajectory for (h_pre,b) obeys

    2[f(x_(k+1)(b))-f(x_star)]/L
             >= q_S+kappa_S(b-b_S)^2.                    (3)

Equality with q_S holds at b=b_S, which is therefore the unique exact-line-
search minimizer for this one function after the supplied prefix. In particular,

    inf_(b>=0) W_(k+1)(h_pre,b) >= q_S.                   (4)

The complete fixed-prefix maximal-interpolation game for the particular jets
constructed below has EXACT optimized normalized value q_S. Thus (4) is backed
by one fixed realizable prefix and a tight terminal completion, not merely by
choosing a different scalar adversary after observing b.

For the Zhang--Jiang bridge notation in the project, b_S=psi(S,0). This is
a final-stage bound depending on the actual prefix sum S; it asserts no bound
on that sum in terms of k and does not prove the all-schedule exact profile.

## 2. The fixed two-dimensional function

It suffices first to set L=1. Define the even scalar Huber function A_S and
the one-sided capped quadratic C_S by

    A_S(u)=u^2/2                         if |u|<=q_S,
           q_S|u|-q_S^2/2               if |u|>=q_S;

    C_S(u)=u^2/2                         if u<=c_S,
           c_S u-c_S^2/2                if u>=c_S.

Their derivatives are respectively the clamp of u to [-q_S,q_S] and min(u,c_S).
They are continuous, nondecreasing, and 1-Lipschitz. The functions are convex
and differentiable, nonnegative, and minimized at zero.

Use the fixed product function and initial point

    F(u,v)=omega_S A_S(u/sqrt(omega_S))
              +(1-omega_S) C_S(v/sqrt(1-omega_S)),

    x_0=(sqrt(omega_S),sqrt(1-omega_S)).                   (5)

Its gradient is 1-Lipschitz: each coordinate gradient has Lipschitz constant
at most one in that coordinate, so the sum of squared gradient differences
is at most the squared Euclidean displacement. It is convex, differentiable,
and has minimum zero at the origin. The initial squared distance is exactly
one. The two scalar coordinates evolve independently under the SAME supplied
stepsizes, starting at scalar value one in both coordinates.

## 3. Every supplied prefix step is legal, in its actual order

Let P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t for 0<=i<=k. Nonnegativity gives 0<=P_i<=S. In the two
scalar coordinates the entire prefix trajectory is

    u_i=1-q_S P_i,    grad A_S(u_i)=q_S,
    v_i=1-c_S P_i,    grad C_S(v_i)=c_S.                  (6)

Indeed c_S=1/(S+1) gives v_i>=c_S, including equality at i=k. Also

    q_S(S+1)=t_S/(2t_S+1)<1,

so u_i>=1-q_S S>q_S. Thus the derivative formulas justify (6) inductively
under every step, regardless of its magnitude or its position in the prefix.
Zero steps merely repeat the same legal data; no strictness assumption occurs.

After the prefix the two scalar coordinates are

    u_k=1-q_S S,    v_k=c_S.

For a final step b>=0 they become

    u_(k+1)(b)=1-q_S(S+b),
    v_(k+1)(b)=c_S(1-b).                                (7)

The second coordinate always belongs to the quadratic region u<=c_S, including
arbitrarily negative values. Consequently its normalized error curve is exactly

    B(b):=2C_S(v_(k+1)(b))=c_S^2(1-b)^2.                 (8)

Let A(b):=2A_S(u_(k+1)(b)). This is a convex differentiable function of b,
being a convex differentiable scalar function composed with an affine map.

## 4. A uniform bound against every final step

At b=b_S the identities (1)-(2) give

    u_(k+1)(b_S)=(1+q_S)/2>q_S,
    A(b_S)=q_S,
    A'(b_S)=-2q_S^2,

    v_(k+1)(b_S)=-c_S t_S,
    B(b_S)=q_S,
    B'(b_S)=2c_S^2 t_S.                                (9)

Convexity supplies a GLOBAL tangent lower bound

    A(b)>=q_S-2q_S^2(b-b_S)                             (10)

for every b>=0, even when (7) has left the Huber linear region or crossed zero.
The exact quadratic (8) expands as

    B(b)=q_S+2c_S^2 t_S(b-b_S)+c_S^2(b-b_S)^2.          (11)

The choice of omega_S in (1) is precisely

    omega_S q_S^2=(1-omega_S)c_S^2 t_S.                 (12)

Multiplying (10) by omega_S and (11) by 1-omega_S cancels the linear terms.
For the ACTUAL single product function (5), its normalized error is
omega_S A(b)+(1-omega_S)B(b). We obtain

    2F(x_(k+1)(b))
       >=q_S+(1-omega_S)c_S^2(b-b_S)^2
        =q_S+kappa_S(b-b_S)^2.

At b=b_S both channel values equal q_S, so the error is exactly q_S.
Since kappa_S>0, this minimizer is unique. This proves (3) and (4) for L=1.
For arbitrary L take f=L F; the iteration x_next=x-(h/L)grad f(x) is the same
normalized iteration, and the division by L in (3) cancels the rescaling.

The terminal gradients also expose why the construction resists line search:

    g_k=(sqrt(omega_S)q_S, sqrt(1-omega_S)c_S),
    g_(k+1)=(sqrt(omega_S)q_S,
                         -sqrt(1-omega_S)c_S t_S).

Equation (12) makes their inner product zero, while the objective stays
q_S/2>0. Both coordinates belong to one fixed legal function throughout.

## 5. The maximal completion of this full prefix is also tight

Apply fact1252b8823bb5a290 to ALL the queried prefix jets of (5), including
their actual values, and let F_max be the pointwise maximal interpolant.
It is legal and agrees with F on every queried point. Since its gradient
at x_k is the same, its possible final points are still (7). Pointwise
maximality and (3) imply

    2F_max(x_(k+1)(b))>=q_S+kappa_S(b-b_S)^2.             (13)

We prove equality at b_S directly by its conjugate construction. Let w be
the terminal gradient of F at b_S, and use the usual prefix y_j=x_j-g_j
and delta_j=f_j-||g_j||^2/2. The two outgoing terminal residuals are

    Q_(k+1,star)=Q_(k+1,k)=0.                           (14)

For the Huber coordinate, both the last query and the terminal point lie
on the same positive linear region, giving equality in both inequalities.
For the capped coordinate, the last query is c_S and the terminal point
is negative, both lying in its quadratic region, again giving equality.
Weighted addition of the two scalar residuals yields (14).

All other prefix residuals with this terminal sample are nonpositive
because F is an actual smooth-convex function. Thus in

    H(v)=||v||^2/2+max_j(<y_j,v>-delta_j),

the optimum and last-query planes are both active at v=w, and no other
prefix plane has a larger value. Directly from (6),

    y_k=(sqrt(omega_S)[1-q_S(S+1)],0).

Set alpha_k=2t_S/(2t_S+1) and alpha_star=1/(2t_S+1). The identity

    x_(k+1)(b_S)=w+alpha_k y_k                          (15)

holds in both coordinates. In the second coordinate it states equality
of the position and gradient in the quadratic region. In the first it
reduces to

    1-q_S(S+b_S+1)
        =[2t_S/(2t_S+1)] [1-q_S(S+1)],

which follows by substituting (1)-(2).

Since (15) is a convex combination of gradients of the two active
quadratics at w, it makes x_(k+1)(b_S) a subgradient of H at w. This can
also be checked by taking the weighted sum of their quadratic supporting
inequalities, with the displayed nonnegative weights. Therefore w is
the conjugate maximizer at this point. Equality (14) identifies its value
as F(x_(k+1)(b_S))=q_S/2. Hence F_max has the same value there.

Together with (13), this proves the exact optimized fixed-prefix maximal
value q_S, using the accepted fact rather than an unverified minimax exchange.
All earlier queries remain in H, even though the two stated planes suffice
to certify the terminal equality for this PARTICULAR construction.

## 6. Quantitative use and precise limit

This gives a dimension-two function depending only on the fixed prefix sum S
whose error is bounded below uniformly over an arbitrary final step. Its
quadratic excess also gives the necessary condition

    W_(k+1)(h_pre,b)<=q_S+epsilon
       => |b-b_S|<=sqrt(epsilon/kappa_S)

for every epsilon>=0. No randomization or adaptive algorithmic choice is
being substituted for the predetermined schedule; (5) is one deterministic
function and works for all b at once.

For S=0 the formulas give t_S=1/2, b_S=3/2, q_S=1/4,
omega_S=8/9, and kappa_S=1/9, recovering an explicit one-step line-search
obstruction. Leading, interior, and trailing zero steps in the prefix do
not alter this conclusion.

For larger prefixes q_S~1/(2S). Consequently this construction alone does
not control an unusually large prefix sum, does not rule out arbitrary
remote forward edges, and does not yield the required exact-profile bound
as a function of k. Its role is a quantitative final-stage boundary
condition for a recurrence that must still constrain the COMPLETE earlier
trajectory. It complements the general minimax candidate and the three-anchor
obstruction without assuming either unverified result in its proof.
